The lice in this country were neither red nor democrats nor poor; they were the result of the war your political predecessors brought to this country, Mr Coello; that is, that they stop telling lies and stop insulting in this House. [[Applauds]] The People's Party is bringing us a motion for an inquiry into the situation in the primary sector. At the very least, it must be said that this motion goes wrong, first of all, and goes wrong because most of the proposals, precisely, result and insist on a model that is at the root of the structural problems of our primary sector. They are also late because, of course, this government is already legislating and is taking out specific packages of measures to solve the short-term problems caused by the crisis in Ukraine that is suffering from our primary sector. Thirdly, they come - to put it this way - at a crawl, because it is the People's Party of Minister García Tejerina, the minister of fertilizer lobbies, the minister who - by the way, now that we are in the worst energy crisis - is sitting in Iberdrola, who comes here to talk about how to save the primary sector. That said, and whatever it is, they bring us a truffle motion of half-truths, of fake news, in which we try to collate, with a sort of disaster drawer - already said here today, more than thirty points - proposals deeply regressive. Firstly, regressive proposals, because they try to dismantle policies to combat climate change, which is a total consensus today; and secondly, regressive proposals because they can only seek to overthrow the resilience and sustainability of our primary sector. But look, just so it is explained that at this time, where there is a total consensus in the European Union on the need for a transition, to abandon agribusiness, to go to new models of agricultural and livestock production, you propose to end the European Green Pact, to end the Farm Strategy at the table, to end the Biodiversity Strategy 2030, and you would just miss joining VOX and Trump and saying that we leave the FAO and also abandon the UN Panel on Climate Change. In short - and we are going to the issue of the 20th and mobilizations - as much as the right and the extreme right try to infiltrate and have tried to capitalize on those mobilizations, in Unity We can say very clearly that we are next, that we listen, that we understand the just demands of the primary sector. And we also say that there were organisations like COAG, such as UPA, with which we and we usually work with those that, by the way, we take forward, from the hands of those organisations and other parliamentary groups, the food chain law, to which you opposed. And that being said, just as we are clear that the mobilizations are not against the government - and this is important - it must be said that the mobilizations are against a model, which is precisely the model of agribusiness, of farmers in the hall, of young ladies and gentlemen who you, gentlemen of the People's Party and of VOX, have been protecting in this country for many years. In Unity We are very clear that we have been warning for a long time that the fundamental problem is to put the food chain in the hands of multinationals, agribusiness and speculative investment funds. We have also been saying for a long time that we must urgently address the weaknesses of our agri-food production model, protecting small and medium-sized farms, guaranteeing supply and thus guaranteeing food sovereignty. But it also draws attention - as you have also said - to how you propose an extraordinary 5 billion fund and raise the 65 million reserve fund of the crisis by 200%, while, systematically, you are telling us that we must lower, lower and lower taxes on all sides. Where do you want to get these resources for our primary sector, ladies and gentlemen of the People's Party? I am now finishing, ladies and gentlemen, with another question that happened today. Today we had to systematically listen to Mr Abascal talk about climate fanaticism. I think it is necessary to say that fanaticism, superchery and lies are, precisely, against all scientific evidence, to deny climate change. So, ladies and gentlemen of the right, of the People's Party and of VOX, say, once and for all, what interests they defend and, above all, not tell us thirty points, but what model they defend for the primary sector of this country.
